115年 10月賦稅收入初步統計 
一、整體賦稅收入 
（一）實徵淨額 
10 月實徵淨額 5,669 億元，較上年同月增加 1,495 億元；累計 1 至
10 月實徵 4 兆 1,383 億元，較上年同期增加 8,499 億元(+25.8%)，
主要由證交稅、營所稅及營業稅所貢獻。隨新版財政收支劃分法實
施，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率由 61.2%降為 4.5%(獲配稅額隨之減
少 2,662 億元)，致中央政府稅收增加數 3,906 億元(+15.8%)，不及
地方政府增加數 4,671 億元(+68.5%)。 
（二）預算達成率 
累 計1 至 10 月 實 徵 淨 額 占 全 年 預 算 數 91.4% ， 其 中 中 央 政 府 為
92.8%，地方政府為87.7%；與全年預算數差距分別為3,913億元、
2,227億元、1,614億元。 
圖 1 賦稅實徵淨額及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）10 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營利事業所得稅增加 1,133 億元、
證券交易稅增加 220 億元及綜合所得稅增加 120 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,297
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 2,986 億元及營業稅增加 1,272 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-10 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)10 月實徵淨額 4,144 億元，較上年同月增加 1,133 億元(+37.6%)，
主因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 1 兆 4,172 億元，較上年同期增加 2,986
億元(+26.7%)，主因結算申報自繳、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款
陸續入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
10 月實徵淨額 614 億元，較上年同月增加 120 億元(+24.3%)，主因
扣繳稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 8,894 億元，較上年同
期增加 910 億元(+11.4%)，主因結算申報自繳稅額增加，以及盈餘分

配、薪資所得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
(1)10月受進口代徵稅額增加及適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅增加
之交互影響，營業稅實徵淨額為負77億元，較上年同月(負146億元)
增加69億元。
(2)累 計1 至10 月 實 徵 淨額6,153 億 元 ， 較 上年 同 期 增 加1,272 億元
(+26.1%)，主因內需穩定，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件等進口
代徵稅額增加，惟外銷營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
10月實徵淨額73億元，較上年同月減少41億元(-35.6%)，累計1至10
月實徵淨額948億元，較上年同期減少248億元(-20.7%)，主因新購汽
機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增加，加以汽柴油減徵幅度擴大所致。
5.證券交易稅
(1)10 月及累計 1 至 10 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 1,210
億元、1 兆 1,437 億元，分別較上年同期(6,267 億元、4,632 億元)
增 78.9%、1.5 倍。
(2)10 月證交稅實徵淨額 523 億元， 較上年同月增加 220 億元
(+72.6%)；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 5,583 億元，較上年同期增加
3,297 億元(+1.4 倍)。
6.關稅
10 月實徵淨額 132 億元，較上年同月增加 7 億元(+6.0%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 1,356 億元，較上年同期增加 88 億元(+6.9%)，均因機
械及電機設備、基本金屬、塑橡膠及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
10 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月減少 11 億元(-31.9%)，主因大額

稅款案件減少；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 234 億元，較上年同期增加
32 億元(+15.9%)，主因大額稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
10 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+5.9%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 560 億元，較上年同期減少
11 億元(-2.0%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.使用牌照稅
10 月實徵淨額 6 億元，較上年同月減少 1 億元(-10.2%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 676 億元，較上年同期減少 1 億元(-0.1%)，主因開徵
輛數減少，以及稅款入帳時間差異所致。
（三）前 10月主要稅目實徵淨額占全年預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%(已提前達成全年預算目標)者有遺產稅(123%)、贈與稅
(107%)、印花稅(106%)、房屋稅(105%)及營所稅(102%)。
2.低於 100%者有牌照稅(99%)、營業稅(94%)、綜所稅(93%)、契稅
(90%)、關稅(88%)、菸酒稅(81%)、證交稅(80%)、貨物稅(79%)、土

增稅(71%)及特銷稅(47%)等。

### Page 4

4
三、政府別賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 10 月中央政府賦稅收入(不含中央統籌分配稅款)實徵淨
額 2 兆 8,579 億元(占全年預算數 92.8%)，較上年同期增加 3,906 億元
(+15.8%)，主因新版財政收支劃分法施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率
大幅調降，獲配稅額隨之減少 2,662 億元，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加
3,297 億元、3,306 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 10 月地方政府賦稅
收入(含中央統籌分配稅款)實
徵淨額 1 兆 1,487 億元(占全年
預算數 87.7%)，較上年同期增
加 4,671 億元(+68.5%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配之營業稅
增加 4,013 億元所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 10 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 220 億元，較
上年同期增加 4 億元(+1.7%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 144
億元，較上年同期減少 6 億元(-4.0%)；撥入長照基金 953 億元(其中 575

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、220 億元來自菸稅，158 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.2%)。

（出所） LSEG、ブルームバーグより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 11.10
先週（11/2〜11/9）発表の主な経済指標
⽶国雇⽤は安定も、AI曝露度の⾼い職業で若年雇⽤が弱含
み。雇⽤不安が⺠主党躍進を後押しした可能性。AI関連株が
調整するなか、韓国中銀は投資減速のアジアへの波及を警戒
所定内給与は物価上昇率を上回る伸びが持続。冬季ボーナス
も押し上げ。電気・ガス料⾦補助再開は消費の追い⾵に。⼀⽅、
消費回復が進めば基調的な物価上昇圧⼒を強める可能性
注目点② 賃上げの波及と物価対策が日本の消費を下支え注目点① 米AI投資拡大の裏で広がる「不安」と「リスク」
⾮農業部⾨雇⽤者数は市場予想を上回り、失業
率も低下。ISM景況指数は製造業・⾮製造業とも
に拡⼤圏を維持。雇⽤・企業活動の底堅さの⼀⽅
で、消費者マインドは持ち直すも依然として低⽔準

欧州

受注ともに振るわず。原材料価格指数は低下も、な
お⾼⽔準。⼈⺠銀⾏は政策⾦利をリバースレポ翌⽇

市場
予想
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
失業率
米国：雇用統計は良好な結果。一方で、賃金上昇もインフレ見合いで見劣り
 10月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋16.5万人と市場予想（同＋6.5万人）を大きく上回る結果
―過去2カ月も合わせて8.4万人の上方修正。業種別では専門ビジネスサービスやヘルスケアが全体をけん引
― 時間当たり賃金も前月比＋0.4％（前年比＋3.1％）と着実に増加。ただしインフレ率に割り負けている状況が継続
 失業率は4.1％と前月（4.2％）から小幅低下も、失業期間は長期化
― 失業期間の中央値は11.9週（9月：同11.5週）と長期化。27週以上の長期失業者も増加基調維持。企業の採用率は低下

傾向が続いており、労働者が失業状態から脱しにくい傾向が継続
3
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

日本：物価対策の再開が家計負担を軽減。消費の回復を後押し
9
 政府は冬場の電気・ガス料金補助の再開を決定。標準的な家庭で計7,000円程度の負担軽減を見込む
― 一方、ガソリン価格の補助は段階的に縮小（基準額を毎月5円ずつ引き上げ）。電気・ガス料金補助金の効果を一部減殺
 補助金を巡る政策変更は、コアCPIの前年比を最大0.4％程度押し下げ
― 賃金上昇に加え、政策による物価負担軽減が家計の実質購買力を下支え
― 物価対策がCPIを押し下げる一方、消費回復を通じて基調的な物価上昇圧力が強まる可能性には留意が必要

（注） ガソリンと同額の補助を実施している軽油・重油・灯油、ガソリン補助額の4割相当
の支援を実施している航空機燃料については未定

中国：政策金利をリバースレポ翌日物に変更。銀行貸出金利の新たな決定メカニズムを模索
 中国人民銀行は政策金利をリバースレポ翌日物に変更（11/2）。短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙い
― リバースレポ7日物から切り替え、翌日物のオペを毎営業日実施。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）も翌日物金利を参照へ
― インターバンク市場における債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）が誘導目標と明言。短期金利の安定化を図る考え
 銀行貸出金利の新たな決定メカニズムを模索。DR001を参照して貸出金利を決定する方法を試験的に導入

― 背景には、銀行貸出の指標金利であるLPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、LPRより低利での貸出比率が上昇している実態
― 今後、財務力のある企業は銀行と協議の上でDR001かLPRを選択し、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを適用へ

中国：景況感は再び悪化し、2カ月ぶりの節目割れ。製造業は需給ともに振るわず
 10月の景況感は再び悪化。総合PMIは49.7と前月（50.7）から大幅に低下し、2カ月ぶりの節目割れ
― うち非製造業PMIが49.7と前月（50.2）から低下も、宿泊やスポーツ・娯楽が持ち直し、情報サービスは堅調だった
― 建設業PMIは48.0と前月（50.3）から急低下。中国物流購買聯合会は建設工事の着工が遅れ気味と指摘
 製造業PMIは49.3と前月（50.1）から低下。供給側の生産、需要側の受注がともに振るわず

― 生産が49.8（前月：51.7）、新規受注が49.4（同：50.5）、新規輸出受注も48.7（同：50.0）といずれも節目の50を下回る
― 中東情勢に伴う価格上昇に一服感。原材料価格は56.8（同：60.8）、出荷価格は51.7（同：54.0）まで戻す

中国：PPIは生産財が急上昇も、川下への価格転嫁進まず。CPIは食品価格が下押し要因に
 10月のPPIは前年比＋5.7％（前月：同＋5.0％）。中東情勢の影響による資源・エネルギー関連業種の価格上昇続く
― 石油・石炭加工（同＋29.9％）と非鉄金属加工（同＋18.6％）、化学品（同＋16.4％）が引き続き高い伸び
― 川上の生産財が同＋7.4％まで上昇も、川下の消費財は同▲0.1％と下落継続。価格転嫁は一向に進まず
 CPIは同＋0.7％と、前月（同＋0.9％）から減速。食品価格の下落（▲2.9％）が下押し要因に（▲0.49％Pt）

― 他方、燃料価格高騰により、ガソリンは同＋22.4％（前月：同＋16.6％）まで上昇し、全体を＋0.63％Pt押し上げ
― 食品・エネルギーを除くコアは同＋0.8％（前月：＋1.0％）と弱いが、通信機器はメモリー高騰で同＋16.1％の伸び

### Page 15

アジア：ASEAN5の製造業景況感は概ね堅調。原油高など仕入コスト上昇の影響は残存
 ASEAN5の10月の製造業景況感は、フィリピンを除き改善圏。受注・生産は好調も、コスト高が継続
― タイ、ベトナムは高水準で推移し、インドネシアは回復が鮮明。一方、フィリピンが基準となる50を下回る水準
― S&Pはリリース資料において、新規受注の増加が生産を支えたものの、仕入コスト上昇と原料調達・物流の制約を指摘
 ASEAN5では、生産者物価（PPI）が高止まり。原油高の影響が残存
― 原油高が続く中、石炭・石油に加えて化学やプラ・ゴム等の製品価格にも上昇圧力が継続
― 仕入コストの高止まりが、企業収益や生産活動の重荷となるリスクに留意が必要
14

### Page 16

 ASEAN5の一部の国で財政収支が悪化
― ベトナム、マレーシア、インドネシアで改善した一方で、フィリピンとタイで財政赤字が拡大し、明暗が分かれる結果に
― フィリピンは税収の伸び悩み、タイは景気下支えに向けた公共投資拡大や予算執行の加速などにより歳出が大きく増加
 各国とも財政面の制約を抱え、追加的な景気下支え余地は限定的
― タイは公的債務残高が法定上限（GDP比70％）に接近。マレーシアも政府債務残高が高水準
― フィリピンは財政赤字の縮小が課題。インドネシアは財政赤字上限（GDP比3％）が制約要因
アジア：ASEAN5の財政収支は明暗が分かれるも、追加的な財政出動余地は限定的

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 27 日
上週重點訊息
◼ AI 投資回報疑慮升溫，資金轉向債市避險：市場對大型科技企業 AI 基礎建設投資規模、融資
需求及未來獲利回收能力的疑慮升溫，導致科技股賣壓擴大，10 月 22 日 Nasdaq 指數下跌
約 2.6%。隨著市場風險偏好下降，避險資金轉向美國公債，10 年期公債殖利率單日下降約 19
個基點，創 2023 年 3 月以來最大單日跌幅，並於 23 日回升至 4.99%。市場關注焦點逐漸由
AI 投資規模轉向資本支出效益，後續大型科技企業財報及現金流表現，將成為評估 AI 產業評

價合理性的重要依據。
◼ 中東衝突推升通膨風險：中東衝突持續升溫，伊朗支持的葉門胡塞武裝攻擊沙烏地阿拉伯，引
發市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂，推升布蘭特原油價格於 10 月 23 日升至每桶 116.72 美元，單
週上漲約 6.2%，創 2022 年以來最高週收盤水準。能源價格攀升加劇全球通膨壓力，並壓抑
市場風險偏好。
◼ 法國政治動盪加劇財政疑慮，歐洲主權債風險重新受到關注：法國因生活成本上升爆發大規模
抗議，進一步加深市場對政府財政整頓能力的疑慮。10 月 19 日法國 10 年期公債殖利率上升
約 10 個基點至 4.88%附近，與德國公債利差擴大至約 144 個基點，創 2012 年以來最高收盤
水準，法股單日下跌約 1.5%，歐元同步走弱。市場擔憂政治不確定性可能削弱財政改革進度，
並透過主權債評價影響銀行業資產負債表。後續須關注法國財政政策、歐洲主權債利差及銀行
融資成本變化。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線宜審慎，中期聚焦基本面穩健的 AI 企業：近期 AI 資本支出、融資需求及評價調整

使科技股波動加劇，但先進製程與半導體設備需求仍具韌性，AI 中長期成長趨勢未改變。隨市
場焦點由成長題材轉向投資效益，企業營收與訂單能見度、自由現金流及資本效率的重要性提
升，具技術優勢、財務穩健及獲利能見度的企業相對有利，而高度依賴外部融資者宜審慎看待。
◼ 美債避險功能重現，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：市場風險偏好降溫時，美國公債仍具一定
避險效果。儘管 Fed 貨幣政策路徑與能源價格增添利率不確定性，短天期債券易受政策利率
牽動，超長天期債券面臨財政赤字、國債供給及期限溢價等壓力，相較之下，中天期美債的風
險報酬較為均衡。信用債方面，投資等級企業基本面及現金流仍具韌性，後續仍可持續關注。

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 09 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 期中選舉「藍色浪潮」，AI 基建政策風險升溫：11 月 3 日美國期中選舉民主黨奪回參眾兩院，
德州新任州長揚言暫停資料中心建設，市場擔憂電力、用水與環境審查趨嚴，AI 資本支出前景
遭重新評價；加上 Fed 官員釋出續升息訊號，資金撤出高評價半導體與 AI 基建族群，美股科
技股大幅修正。短線政策雜音難消，但 AI 需求本質未變，後續觀察新國會實際立法方向。
◼ 科技財報喜憂參半，台股賣壓沉重、政府出手穩市：蘋果營收優於預期但毛利率受記憶體成本

壓抑，AMD 資料中心營收近倍增、高通財報後走弱。美股賣壓延燒，台股連日重挫、新台幣
貶破 33 元，行政院指示金管會機動因應並研議穩定股市措施，官股行庫與投信持續買超。看
法：短線波動仍大，但基本面與政策支撐有助指數逐步築底。
◼ 美國製造業續擴張，台灣通膨降溫：美國 10 月 ISM 製造業指數升至 55.5，連續第十個月擴
張，惟價格分項仍處高檔；台灣 10 月製造業 PMI 連續 13 個月擴張但速度趨緩，CPI 年增率
降至 2.33%。看法：美國景氣與通膨黏性支撐 Fed 偏鷹立場，台灣央行壓力相對減輕；後續
關注 10 月非農就業、11 月 10 日美國 CPI 及 12 月 FOMC 會議。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，
惟短線宜重視個股分化，美國期中選舉帶來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，
惟大型雲端業者持續投入 AI 基礎建設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，科技股整
體基本面仍具支撐，但短期應避免過度集中於高評價、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企

業。優先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業。
◼ 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價調整期
間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延
長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置
穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人
仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

強茂(2481 TT) Buy

車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨帶動，投資建議 Buy。  
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資
料中心與 AI 伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 
Buy 的投資建議，目標價 160 元。 
車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨，3Q26 獲利三率三升：  
強茂 3Q26 營收 43.50 億元（QoQ +10.0%、 YoY +33.0%），主
因車用及 AI 需求帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合
優化提升至 37.3%（ QoQ +4.1 ppts、 YoY +6.0 ppts）；營業利益 
6.97 億元（QoQ +50.1%、 YoY +152.3%）；稅後淨利 6.49 億
元（QoQ +33.0%、 YoY +114.0%），3Q26 EPS 為 1.70 元。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高（YOY+2ppts），
車用產品營收較去年同期大幅成長 47.2%。AI 與雲端運算營收
佔整體營收逾 12%，拉貨將逐步提升。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及
五座後段封測廠，隨著 2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加
入營運，進一步強化東南亞供應能力。預估 2026 年營收 
160.48 億元（YoY +22.6%），EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提
升：隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗提高，強茂成功切入美系 CSP 的  
ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈，主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）
與 ORing（冗餘電源管理兩大關鍵應用），推動 AI 佔比朝 15%
以上邁進。車用方面，除了 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放
量外，更推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日歐美市場。綜上所
述，預估強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元（YoY +19.8%），EPS 
8.41 元。基於車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器新世代平台拉貨需求攀
升，給予 Buy 投資建議，目標價 160 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
在  ESG 永續發展表現上，強茂積極落實環境保護、供應鏈管理與綠色技術
創新三大面向： 
環境永續：持續投入節能減碳與能源轉型，透過製程節能改善及再生能源建
置，2025 年全年節電約 91.1 萬度，減少約 432 公噸 CO₂e 排放；太陽能系
統全年發電量突破 263 萬度，穩健提升綠電使用比重並朝向低碳營運邁進。
責任供應鏈：依循責任商業聯盟(RBA)標準深化供應鏈管理，年度完成 75 家
供應商 RBA 稽核且合格率達 100%，攜手合作夥伴打造具韌性的永續價值
鏈。 
創新與產品發展：持續深耕高效率功率半導體研發，2025 年集團累計有效
專利達 102 件，並維持 90 分以上的客戶滿意度，以高效能半導體解決方案
協助終端客戶提升能源使用效率，共同響應全球淨零碳排趨勢。

公司簡介： 
強茂成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體領導廠商之一，主要從事離散元件
（ Discrete）之研發、製造與銷售，產品涵蓋 MOSFET、Schottky、Rectifier、
TVS、 Zener、 Bridge Transistor、 ESD Protection 及  SiC 等。公司母公司專
注於核心元件開發，並透過關係企業與子公司進行垂直與水平整合，包含熒
茂光學、凡甲科技、虹冠電子、汎星半導體及新貿科技。強茂在低功率矽整
流器領域位居全球第四大供應商，並持續聚焦車用電子、雲端資料中心與 AI
伺服器等高附加價值應用。 
主要競爭對手分析 
1. 國際一線 IDM 大廠（高階車用與 AI 伺服器主要對手） 
強茂近年積極搶攻歐美車廠與 AI 機櫃市佔，主要在以下領域與國際巨頭
正面交鋒（並受惠於供應鏈去中化或對手產能受限之轉單效應）： 
• 安世半導體（Nexperia）：最直接的車用與小訊號元件競爭對手。安
世在車用小訊號二極體、ESD/TVS 保護元件及中低壓 MOSFET 市
佔率極高；每當安世出現產能吃緊或地緣政治/出口管制干擾時，歐美
Tier 1 車廠往往優先將訂單轉移至強茂。 
• 意法半導體（STMicroelectronics） 、 安森美（onsemi） 、 英飛凌
（ Infineon） ：全球車用與工業功率半導體前三大巨頭，在車規
MOSFET、SiC（碳化矽）及 AI 伺服器電源管理擁有完整生態系。強
茂以高性價比及交期彈性切入其二供（Second Source）體系。 
• 威世（Vishay）、達爾（Diodes Incorporated）、羅姆（ROHM）：
在整流二極體、蕭特基二極體及中低壓 MOSFET 產品線高度重疊，
是強茂在美系與日系客戶端的主要比價與份額競爭者。 
2. 台灣分離式元件同業 
• 台半（5425）：在整流二極體與車用 MOSFET 布局深厚（切入 Tier

                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

4 群益投顧 
帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合優化提升至 37.3% ，
QoQ+4.1ppts，YoY+6.0ppts；營業利益 6.97 億元，QoQ+50.1%，YoY+152.3%，
呈現大幅提升；稅後淨利 6.49 億元，QoQ+32.96%， YoY+113.95%， 3Q26 
EPS 1.70 元，3Q26 財報表現亮眼。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高，年增 2 ppts，車用產品營收
較去年同期大幅成長 47.2%。 AI 與雲端運算營收佔整體營收逾 12%，受惠
新世代平台規格推進拉貨穩健。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及五座後段封測廠，隨
2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加入營運，進一步強化東南亞第三供
應能力，2H26 營運持續增長，4Q26 營運預估與 3Q26 持平，預估 2026 年
營收 160.48 億元(YOY+22.56%)， EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提升： 
強茂本身具有歐美車廠供應鏈能力，在安世半導體產能與出貨受阻後，客戶
端為確保供貨安全，加速將小訊號二極體與保護元件（ESD/TVS）訂單轉移
至強茂，使推升其車用營收比重與整體市佔率。隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗
大幅提高，資料中心對供電穩定度與電源轉換效率的要求越發嚴苛。在成功
切入美系 CSP（雲端服務大廠）的 ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈後，
強茂主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）與 ORing（冗餘電源管理）兩大關鍵應用。這
類高電流供電路徑需要具備「超低導通電阻（Low RDS(on)）」的中高壓
MOSFET 來降低功耗與發熱，其 ASP 遠高於傳統消費性元件，預估 AI 佔比
可朝 15%以上邁進。 
車用展望方面，目前佔強茂整體營收比重約 35%。隨著電動車與智慧座艙滲
透率提升，車內馬達驅動、電池管理系統（BMS）及車載充電器對 MOSFET 
的用量呈倍數增長。強茂除了既有的 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放量
外，更進一步推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日本與歐美車用市場，產品
線從小訊號保護元件成功延伸至高毛利的動力與電源核心。綜上所述，預估
強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元(YOY+19.8%)， EPS 8.41 元。 
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資料中心與 AI 
伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 Buy 的投資建議，目標價
160 元。

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Shenzhen Goodix Technology

Price Target Rmb58.00 Rmb40.00
Higher memory costs are squeezing handset makers’ budgets for
displays, touch controllers and sensors. We expect weak
Android volumes to limit DDIC suppliers’ ability to pass on
higher wafer and packaging costs, even where new OLED
programs lift content per phone.
Android bears most of the decline. Our US hardware team (2026 AlphaWise

Smartphone Survey: Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In) expects 2027
smartphone shipments to fall 5%, versus 4% growth previously ( Exhibit 2 ). The
team's bear case is a global decline of 11%. The base case has Android down about
9% to 768mn units and iPhone up 7% to 270mn. Across the US, China, the UK and
Germany, 41% of owners were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months, down
2ppt ( Exhibit 3 ). The survey implies a replacement cycle of 4.4 years in 2027, up

from 4.2. We expect Android supply chains to face the greatest pressure in 2027:
falling shipments, continued de-specing, and margin compression leave Android
OEMs and suppliers more exposed as brands attempt to offset higher component
costs through a combination of price increases and bill of materials (BOM)
reductions.
China's replacement demand is weakening. We expect a 7% decline in China
smartphone shipments in 2027, following an estimated 12% decline in 2026. Among

China survey respondents, 48% were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months,
down from 49% last year. When asked why they chose their current phone, 17%
cited built-in AI, down from 22%, while 35% cited overall device quality, up from
32% ( Exhibit 4 ).
Brand switching favors Apple in China ( Exhibit 6 ). Among respondents in China,
Apple had the strongest net switching intention at +28% of its installed base. The
corresponding rates for Xiaomi, Oppo, Vivo and Honor ranged from -21% to -31%. A

shift in purchases toward Apple would put further further pressure on component
demand at these four Android brands.
Stock implications: Egis: Rating downgraded to UW and price target lowered from
NT$105 to NT$66; we estimate wider losses in 2026, lower our 2027 EPS estimate
25% and introduce 2028 estimates. Himax: Remain EW, price target down from US
$17.4 to US$9.2, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down 17%/17%/33%. Goodix: Remain
UW, PT down from Rmb58 to Rmb40, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Himax: Earnings Revision and Quarterly Financials
We lower 2026/27/28 EPS estimates 17%/17%/33%: We incorporate 2Q-3Q26 actual
results. We see continued weakness in the consumer market (especially smartphones) due
to elevated component costs and weak demand. Increasing material and foundry prices
might also affect Himax's margins, as we believe it is unlikely to pass through to clients.
However, we expect 2027 to see more contributions from OLED DDIC, OLED T-con

shipments and WiseEye opportunities, which should help revenue and gross margin.
Continued OLED shipments for a mainstream model and inventory builds for new
premium phones lifted combined LCD and OLED handset IC sales in 3Q26. Management
expects smartphone driver sales to decline Q/Q in 4Q26. Its LCD touch-and-display drivers
serve the entry and mid-range segments, in which pressure to cut component costs is
greatest.

Himax: Valuation Methodology
We remain EW and lower our price target to US$9.2: This factors in our lower
expectations for 2026-28 earnings. Our beta assumption is raised from 1.3 to 1.5 (leading
to an increased cost of equity from 9.9% to 11.0%); the increase in beta reflects the higher
uncertainties around end market demand and component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure.
Our terminal growth rate assumption is cut from 3.4% to 2.0%; the new terminal growth

rate is now more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed
economies over the longer term. Other residual income assumptions are unchanged,
including risk-free rate of 2%, risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 8.0%.  We
lower our bull/bear-case values from US$26.0/8.7 to US$13.7/4.6.

Goodix: Estimate Revisions
We  lower our 2026/27/28 earnings estimates 14%/18%/5%. We factor in 2-3Q26 actual
results, and reduce our earnings forecasts to reflect weak smartphone end market
demand and cost pressures.  Smartphones accounted for 62.6% of 1H26 sales, and weaker
demand and chip pricing weighed on 3Q revenue. Xiaomi’s 18 Pro series uses two Goodix
touch controllers and its ultrasonic fingerprint sensor. Wider adoption of integrated OLED

touch and display drivers (TDDI) would displace standalone touch controllers. Goodix’s
own OLED TDDI was still in internal testing in October.

Goodix: Valuation Methodology
We maintain our UW rating. We lower our price target to Rmb40 from Rmb58: This is
based on a residual income model using our base-case forecasts. The lower price target
reflects our lower earnings estimate. We also lower our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.9% to 2.5%. The new terminal growth rate is now more in line with the macro
economic growth rate of global economies over the longer term.

Other key RI assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 8.4%, an
intermediate growth rate of 16.5%. We lower our bull-case value to Rmb55 from Rmb80
and our bear-case value to Rmb21 from Rmb31.

earnings estimates to 2028. We continue to believe Egis will remain loss-making in 2026,

Egis: Valuation methodology
We downgrade our rating to UW and lower our price target from NT$105 to NT$66:
This is our base-case scenario value derived from a residual income model. This reflects
the changes in our 2026-27 earnings estimates. We roll forward our valuation date from
2025 to 2026.
Changes in our RI assumptions include:
• Cost of equity is raised to 11.0% from 9.5% (beta raised from 1.25 to 1.50). The
increase in beta reflects the higher uncertainties around end market demand and

component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure
• Intermediate growth rate cut from 14.8% to 8.0%. The decrease in intermediate
growth rate reflects our more moderate view on the end market demand recovery
and new products ramp up schedule
• Terminal growth rate cut from 5.0% to 2.0%. The new terminal growth rate is now
more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed economies
over the longer term
Other key RI assumptions remain unchanged:

• Risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%
• Payout ratio of 40%
We lower our bull-case value from  NT$143 to NT$95 and bear-case value from NT$49 to
NT$30, implying 24x and 7x 2027 EPS, respectively, on our estimates.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189.TW)
25 November 2026 Citi Research
2
Kinsus Interconnect Technology
Valuation
Our DCF-based target price for Kinsus is NT$950. Factoring in a 5.0% risk-free rate (YTD avg. 10-year government bond), a 7%
risk premium and beta of 1.38, we calculate a WACC of 14.3%. Our DCF model assumes 1.5% long-term revenue growth and a
31.5% EBIT margin in 2028E with gradual margin improvement going forward. We believe the worst of demand/profitability

headwinds are over given recovery demand and more disciplined supply control by the industry. Our DCF-based target price
equates to 26/15X 2027/28E EPS and 7.8x/5.7x 2027/28E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the Kinsus stock from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker smartphone demand.;
2) weaker-than-expected ABF demand momentum or market oversupply; and 3) lower-than-expected contribution from AiP.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Victory Giant is a leading AI PCB supplier, with continued investment in R&D and 
capacity expansion to support long-term growth. We are positive on the company’s 
growth prospects, driven by rising AI infrastructure demand, PCB specification upgrades 
toward higher layer counts and higher-end materials, increasing PCB usage in AI servers 
to replace copper cables, and customer expansion into ASIC servers. We expect 
migration to next-generation AI platforms to support higher dollar content, while 
continued capacity expansion should enable the company to capture growing demand. 
We are Buy-rated on Victory Giant. 
We are Buy-rated on Shennan. The company is a key PCB supplier in China that focuses 
on high-end markets, including, telecom, datacom, automotive, industrials, medicals, 
etc. We see the company as a key beneficiary of China’s domestic AI infrastructure 
investments, and therefore expect a robust revenue and net profit growth trend for 
Shennan as local equipment vendors ramp up their server/networking shipment. Current 
valuation is attractive as it trades at a discount to historical average. 
WUS is a leading global PCB supplier, expanding from telecom equipment, networking 
devices, to high-speed networking in AI data center: 800G / 1.6T switch PCB, switch 
board in rack-level AI servers, midplane / backplane in rack-level AI servers to replace 
copper cables, etc. We are positive on WUS growth ahead, driven by (1) AI infrastructure 
ramp up, (2) PCB specification upgrade along with rising computing density per rack and 
high-speed networking, (3) PCB usage increase per AI servers rack, replacing copper 
cables, easier for assembly, and (4) strong commitment in both R&D and capacity 
expansion, supporting the company’s ability to capture the rising PCB trend. We are 
Buy-rated on WUS. WUS currently trades at 15.8x 2027E P/E and we believe the 
valuation is attractive considering +48% NI YoY on avg. in 2027-28E and OPM up to 26% 
by 2028E from 24% in 9M25.  
Shengyi Tech is a leading copper-clad laminate (CCL) supplier and a key beneficiary of 
growing AI infrastructure demand. We are positive on the company’s long-term growth, 
driven by rising AI server shipments, CCL specification upgrades and continued capacity 
expansion. We expect higher transmission speeds in rack-level AI servers to support 
increasing adoption of M9 and above products, driving product mix improvement, while

strong demand should support AI CCL pricing. We believe AI CCL demand growth can
absorb planned industry capacity additions, supporting continued shipment growth and

Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb540 on a target P/E multiple of 26.3x applied
to our 2027E EPS. Our target P/E of 26.3x is derived from the correlation between P/E
and EPS growth of Victory Giant’s peers, based on the company’s 2028E EPS YoY

growth.
Key risks: Slower-than-expected AI server shipment ramp up pace;
Slower-than-expected AI server PCB specification upgrade; Fiercer-than-expected
market competition.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Shennan Circuits
We are Buy rated on Shennan Circuits with a 12-month target price of Rmb511, which is
based on a 39.7x target P/E multiple on our 2027E EPS.
Downside risks: 1) slower-than-expected expansion to AI PCB; 2) fiercer-than-expected

competition, which could lead to ASP erosion and margin pressure; 3) customer
concentration risk; and 4) slower-than-expected server/automotive PCB and IC
substrate growth.
Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb148 on a target P/E multiple of 23x 2027E EPS.
Our target P/E of 23x is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of
WUS’s peers, based on the company’s 2027E-28E EPS YoY growth.
Key risks: (1) Slower-than-expected high-end AI server and high-speed switch

migration; (2) Fiercer-than-expected competition in AI PCB market; and (3)
Slower-than-expected new capacity expansion.
Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb255 on a target P/E multiple of 50.4x 2027E
EPS. Our target P/E of 50.4x is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth
of Shengyi Tech’s peers, based on the company’s 2027E-28E EPS YoY growth.
Key risks: Lower-than-expected AI infrastructure investment, Lower-than-expected
allocation, Change in the direction of technology.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

◼ 結 論 與 建 議  
基 於 以 下 理 由 ， 研 究 部 給 予 博 智 (8155) 買 進 評 等 ：  
1): 產 品 組 合 優 化 ， 成 本 轉 嫁 帶 動 獲 利 創 高 ： 3Q2 6 下 半 年  ASIC CPU 主
板 、 高 層 板 等 高 階 產 品 出 貨 比 重 提 升 ， 加 上 CCL 漲 價 成 本 順 利 轉 嫁 ， 毛 利
率 提 升 至 26.8% ， 單 季 營 收 、 毛 利 率 與 E PS 同 創 歷 史 新 高 。  
2): 伺 服 器 規 格 升 級 ， 高 階 製 程 優 勢 推 升 AS P： 伺服器 PCB 朝 高 層 數 、 大 尺
寸、N+M 非 對 稱 壓 合 及 HDI 演 進 ， 板 厚 由 5.0m m 朝 6.5mm 發 展 、 材 料
升 級 至 M8~M10。 博 智 具 備 大 尺 寸 板 平 整 度 控 制 、 精 密 背 鑽 及 高 縱 橫 比 電
鍍 等 工 法 ， 產 品 組 合 持 續 朝 高 毛 利 方 向 優 化 。  
3): 越南 ACM 廠 2 Q27 量 產 ， 突 破 產 能 瓶 頸 ： 台 灣 廠 稼 動 率 逾 九 成 ， 與 日
商 Meiko 合 資 之 越 南 ACM 廠 預 計 2Q27 量 產 ， 產 線 規 格 已 全 面 升 級 至 與
台 灣 廠 同 步 ， 可 承 接 伺 服 器 及 工 業 電 腦 高 階 產 品 ； 長 線 布 局 低 軌 衛 星 及 太
空  A I 資 料 中 心 ， 開 拓 新 成 長 曲 線 。  
4): 營 收 獲 利 持 續 成 長 ： 研 究 部 預 估 2026/2027 年 營 收 65 .60/79.2 2 億元，
YoY+32.5% /+20 .8%， 毛 利 率 25 .5%/28 .5%， 營 益 率 16 .3%/18 .1%， 稅 後
淨利 9 .35/11.58 億 元 ， EPS 14.83/18 .38 元 。 考 量 AI 資 料 中 心 建 置 節 奏
轉 趨 理 性 ， 以 2027 年 E PS 給予 17 倍 本 益 比 ， 目 標 價 315 元 ， 投 資 評 等 強
力 買進。 
◼ 重點摘要 
公 司 簡 介  
博 智 電 子 股 份 有 限 公 司 成 立 於 1995 年 4 月 26 日，總 部 位 於 桃 園 市 龍 潭 區，
為 專 業 高 層 數 硬 板 PCB 製 造 商，製 程 能 力 最 大 板 厚 8.0mm、層 數 2 ~7 2 L，
具備 VIPPO、 S kip Via（ L1-L3 la ser  via）、 S in tering、 Press Fit 及 Flip 
Drilling 等 技 術 ， 主 攻 少 量 多 樣 之 高 階 伺 服 器 、 半 導 體 IC 測 試 板 及 工 業 電
腦 市 場 。 主 要 股 東 為 仁 寶 集 團（ 持 股 29.42% ）及 研 華 集 團（ 持 股 5 .7 0 % ），
合計 35 .12%， 策 略 股 東 持 股 穩 定 ； 台 灣 廠 員 工 逾 1,200 人。 
1~3Q26 產 品 組 合 以 層 別 區 分 ， 2L~12L 占 23%， 14L~20L 占 50%，主要
為 Ser ver， 22L~48+L 占 27%， 亦 有 Ser ver、 IPC； 以 應 用 別 區 分 ， Ser ver
占 82%、IC Tester 占 9%、IPC 占 7%、Networking 占 1%、Other s 占 1%。
Ser ver 占比由先前約 90%下 降，主 因 其 他 類 別 增 加，今 年 起 IC Tester 自 IPC
獨立列示。

3Q26 營 運 概 況  
博智 3Q26 營收 16 .81 億 元 ， QoQ +5.5%， Y oY +38.6%； 毛 利 率 2 6 .8 % ，
QoQ+3.0 ppts ， Y oY+6.2ppts ； 營 益 率 18.3 % ， QoQ+3.6 ppts ， YoY  
+6.6 ppts；稅 後 淨 利 2.7 8 億 元，QoQ+34.1%，Y oY+120.5%；EPS 4 .41 元，
單 季 營 收 、 毛 利 率 與  E PS 均 創 歷 史 新 高 。 毛 利 率 大 幅 提 升 主 因 ： （ 1 ）下
半年 AS IC CPU 主 板 、 高 層 板 等 高 階 產 品 出 貨 比 重 拉 高 ， AS P 隨 之 上 揚 ；
（ 2）面 對 上 游 CCL 持 續 漲 價 ， 公 司 採 每 季 與 客 戶 重 議 價 格 機 制 ， 約 八 成 客
戶 可 全 額 吸 收 原 物 料 漲 價 成 本 。  
累計 1~3Q26 營收 48.11 億 元 ， Y oY+35 .0% ，毛利率 24.95% ， 營 益 率
15.8%， 稅 後 淨 利 6.81 億 元 ， YoY +274.5% ， EPS 10.81 元 ， 已 接 近 2022
年 全 年 獲 利 高 點 。 10 月營收 5.64 億 元 ， MoM+0 .1%， Y oY+28 .9%，大致
持平 3Q26 單 月 平 均 水 準 ； 累 計 1~10 月 營 收 53.7 5 億 元 ， YoY +34.3% 。

產 能 擴 廠 進 度  
台 灣 廠 稼 動 率 維 持 90%以 上 ， 新 租 廠 房 已 於 2025~2026 年 陸 續 投 產 ， 受
限 廠 房 空 間 ， 短 期 僅 能 挑 選 產 值 最 高 之 產 品 組 合 ， 例 如 需 6 階 HD I、重複
壓 合 與 電 鍍 6 ~7 次之 AI 加 速 卡，因 電 鍍 線 與 壓 合 機 台 負 載 倍 增，現 階 段 暫
不 承 接 。  
越南 ACM 廠 為 博 智 與 日 商 Meiko 合 資 ， 位 於 河 內 並 緊 鄰 Meiko 廠 區 ， 在
Meik o 當 地 資 源 協 助 下 僅 耗 時 9 個 月 即 完 成 一 期 廠 房，2026 年 8 月 完 成 點
交，9 月 底 設 備 陸 續 進 機 ， 預 計 1Q27 取 得 營 運 執 照 、 2 Q27 正 式 量 產 ， 二
期 廠 房 亦 已 啟 動 打 樁 。 因 應 伺 服 器 規 格 快 速 升 級 ， 公 司 過 去 半 年 重 新 配 置
設 備 ， 將 越 南 廠 由 原 規 劃 之 18 層 以 下 標 準 板 ， 提 升 為 「 台 灣 能 做 什 麼 ， 越
南 就 能 做 什 麼 」 ， 除 IC 測 試 板 保 留 於 台 灣 外 ， 可 全 面 承 接 伺 服 器 與 工 業 電
腦 高 階 產 品 。 人 力 方 面 已 委 託 Meiko 越 南 廠 代 訓 技 術 人 員 ， 相 較 同 業 赴 泰
國 擴 產 面 臨 工 程 延 宕 及 技 術 人 力 短 缺 ， 建 廠 與 量 產 進 度 具 相 對 優 勢 ； 越 南
新 產 能 開 出 後 ， 台 灣 廠 亦 可 釋 出 空 間 承 接 更 高 階 產 品 。

伺 服 器 規 格 升 級 ， 製 程 門 檻 同 步 拉 高  
AI 與 AS IC 伺 服 器 架 構 演 進 快 速 ， N+N、 N+M 非 對 稱 壓 合 、 主 板 HD I 及
大 尺 寸 板 已 逐 步 成 為 主 流 規 格 。 N+M 結 構 因 兩 側 熱 膨 脹 係 數 與 應 力 不 對
稱 ， 易 產 生 板 彎 翹 ， 且 須 在 微 幅 彎 翹 的 超 大 尺 寸 板 面 上 進 行 數 千 至 數 萬 孔
的 精 密 背 鑽 ， S tub 殘 厚 公 差 由 過 去 6 ±4 mil 收斂至 2~6 mil； 高 階 伺 服 器
板 縱 橫 比 已 達 15~16 倍，IC 測 試 板 更 高 達 20 ~3 0 倍 ， 設 備 商 與 藥 水 廠 均
難 以 保 證 良 率 。  
導入 Sequen tia l 及 HDI 製 程 後 ，生 產 站 點 增 加 1.3~1.5 倍 ， 生 產 週 期 同 步
拉 長 ， 使 具 備 穩 定 量 產 能 力 的 板 廠 相 對 有 限 。 博 智 憑 藉 大 尺 寸 板 平 整 度 控
制、非 對 稱 壓 合 及 高 縱 橫 比 電 鍍 等 廠 內 工 法，可 望 受 惠 規 格 升 級 帶 動 之 A SP
提 升 。

高 階 產 品 與 新 應 用 進 度  
◼伺 服 器：A SIC 伺 服 器 成 長 最 為 明 顯，博 智 供 應 CPU 主 板、I/O 板 及 配 卡 ，
ASIC CPU 主 板 為 現 階 段 出 貨 主 力 ； ARM 架構 CPU 板 已 透 過 兩 家 OEM 打
樣 ， 預 計 2027 年 小 量 產 出 。 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 約 6~8 個 月 ， A SIC 客戶下
單 週 期 較 長 。  
◼IC 測 試 板 ： 產 品 涵 蓋 L oad Board 、 Burn -in Board 及 SL T， 客 戶 設 計 已
見 60~70 層、板 厚 逾 10mm 之 需 求，超 高 層 板 可 透 過 單 板 一 次 壓 合 或 兩 片
20 餘 層 板 對 疊 二 次 壓 合 生 產 。 測 試 板 屬 治 具 與 耗 材 性 質 ， 無 IPC 規 範 與 冗
長 驗 證 流 程 限 制 ， 3 Q26 起 既 有 大 型 美 系 專 案 穩 定 出 貨 ， 2027 年 將 再 增 加
數 個 美 系 客 戶 測 試 案 ； 若 取 得 下 一 世 代 專 案 ， 營 收 占 比 有 望 提 升 至 20%。  
◼工 業 電 腦 ： 涵 蓋 工 業 機 器 設 備 、 醫 療 及 機 器 人（ 目 前 以 非 人 形 為 主 ）主 控
板，主 要 客 戶 研 華 接 單 動 能 強 勁，B/B 值 維 持 在 1 以 上 並 提 前 下 長 單 備 料 ，
訂 單 能 見 度 達 6~12 個 月 。  
◼低 軌 衛 星 與 太 空 AI 資 料 中 心 ： 衛 星 天 上 端 用 板 須 耐 受 極 端 溫 差 、 輻 射 與
高 頻 訊 號 完 整 性 考 驗 ， Meiko 越 南 既 有 產 線 以 消 費 性 產 品 為 主 難 以 承 接 ，
未 來 將 由 合 資 之 A CM 廠 提 供 相 關 產 能 ； 太 空 AI 資 料 中 心 架 構 與 地 面 資 料
中 心 大 致 相 同 ， 主 要 差 異 在 耐 太 空 環 境 之 特 殊 材 料 ， 博 智 有 機 會 參 與 ， 預
計 2028 年 起 逐 步 成 為 新 動 能 。

護 城 河 分 析  
1) ：高階 P CB 製 程 技 術 門 檻 高 ， 具 備 差 異 化 競 爭 優 勢 ： 博 智 深 耕 高 層 數 、
高 厚 度 及 大 尺 寸 PCB 市 場 ， 具 備 最 高 72 層 、 板 厚 8.0mm 之 製 程 能 力 ， 並
掌握 N+M 非 對 稱 壓 合、大 尺 寸 板 平 整 度 控 制、精 密 背 鑽 及 高 縱 橫 比 電 鍍 等
關 鍵 技 術 。 博 智 可 憑 藉 長 期 累 積 之 製 程 經 驗 與 良 率 控 制 能 力 ， 建 立 技 術 門
檻 ， 並 受 惠 產 品 規 格 升 級 帶 動 ASP 及 毛 利 率 提 升 。  
2)：策略股東與日商合作，強化客戶資源及海外擴產優勢：博 智 主 要 股 東
包 括 仁 寶 集 團 及 研 華 集 團 ， 合 計 持 股 35.12%， 有 助 公 司 深 化 伺 服 器 及 工 業
電 腦 產 業 鏈 合 作 關 係 。 海 外 布 局 方 面 ， 公 司 與 日 商 Meiko 合 資 設 立 越 南
ACM 廠 ， 借 助 Meik o 當 地 既 有 廠 區 資 源 、 供 應 鏈 及 技 術 人 員 培 訓 體 系 ， 縮
短 新 廠 建 置 時 程 ， 一 期 廠 房 僅 耗 時 9 個 月 即 完 成 ， 預 計 2 Q27 正 式 量 產 。
相 較 同 業 海 外 擴 產 面 臨 工 程 延 宕 及 人 力 不 足 問 題 ， 博 智 透 過 策 略 合 作 降 低
建 廠 執 行 風 險 ， 且 越 南 廠 規 格 與 台 灣 同 步 ， 未 來 可 承 接 高 階 伺 服 器 及 工 業
電 腦 訂 單 ， 進 一 步 強 化 全 球 供 應 能 力 與 長 期 競 爭 優 勢 。

風 險 性 分 析  
1)：越 南 新 廠 量 產 進 度 與 初 期 良 率 風 險：博 智 台 灣 龍 潭 廠 稼 動 率 已 維 持 90 %
以 上 ， 現 有 產 能 限 制 新 客 戶 及 高 階 產 品 導 入 ， 未 來 成 長 高 度 仰 賴 越 南 ACM
廠 擴 產 進 度 。 若 新 廠 量 產 時 程 延 後 、 初 期 良 率 不 如 預 期 ， 或 產 能 利 用 率 爬
升 速 度 低 於 預 估 ， 除 影 響 新 增 訂 單 承 接 能 力 ， 亦 可 能 因 折 舊 及 人 事 成 本 增
加 而 壓 抑 獲 利 表 現 。

影 響 ， 導 致 客 戶 拉 貨 節 奏 調 整 。

摘要：
⼈⼯智能交易回暖提振市场⻛险偏好，全球股市普涨。
周五，美国股指期货全线⾛⾼，预⽰感恩节假期后华尔街的反弹⾏情有望延续。纳斯达克100指
数期货上涨2.5%，标普500指数期货上涨1.7%；MSCI亚太指数上涨1.8%，延续周三美股反弹
势头。
市场⻛险偏好回升，投资者减持避险资产，美国国债价格随之下跌。感恩节假期结束后，美债
现货交易于周五恢复，10年期美债收益率上升6个基点⾄4.92%。美元⼩幅⾛低，美元指数下跌
0.1%，但仍有望创下两年来最⻓的⽉度连涨纪录。
商品市场⽅⾯，⻩⾦价格上涨2.6%，接近每盎司3660美元，现货⽩银涨超6%；布伦特原油涨
1%⾄92.36美元/桶。
Reed Capital Partners⾸席投资官Gerald Gan表⽰：“市场似乎正在企稳，尽管这可能只是短暂

的反弹，但我们的基本预期仍然认为，到年底前市场将迎来⼀波最终的上涨⾏情。”
核⼼市场⾛势如下：
标普500指数期货上涨近2%；纳斯达克100指数期货上涨2.6%。
美股盘前，英伟达涨超4%，Anthropic涨超6%，SpaceX涨超4%；纳指期货盘前时段涨约
2.8%，闪迪美股盘前涨超10%。美光科技盘前涨超6%，SK海⼒⼠涨超6%，特斯拉涨超
2%。

指数持平，德国DAX指数涨0.47%。

### Page 2

抛售潮暂缓，美股期指⾛⾼，AI交易能否持续反弹仍待观察
⼈⼯智能交易能否延续反弹，仍是市场关注的焦点。尽管科技股带动全球股市回暖，但投资者
对AI巨额资本投⼊能否转化为⾜够回报仍存疑虑。经历近期市场剧烈波动后，交易员对进⼀步加
仓保持谨慎，市场复苏的持续性仍有待检验。
科技股成为亚太市场反弹的主要动⼒，也是MSCI亚太指数中表现最强的板块。韩国芯⽚巨头SK
海⼒⼠、三星电⼦以及⽇本铠侠控股股价均有所上涨，显⽰AI产业链仍是资⾦关注的核⼼。
Astris Advisory Japan战略主管Neil Newman表⽰，当前AI和半导体交易似乎仍处于停滞状态，
市场尚未完全摆脱⻛险。他指出，近期市场动荡加剧了投资者的谨慎情绪，AI相关资产能否真正
企稳仍不明朗。
⻛险偏好回升削弱避险需求，美债收益率全线上⾏
股市情绪回暖削弱了投资者对避险资产的需求，美国国债价格随之下跌，收益率曲线各期限美
债收益率均⾄少上升3个基点。美国感恩节假期结束后，美债现货交易于周五恢复。
道明证券新加坡策略师Prashant Newnaha表⽰，⻛险偏好回升是推动美债收益率上⾏的主要因

素。周三美国科技股（周四美股因感恩节休市）尾盘的反弹势头延续⾄亚洲交易时段，进⼀步
提振了市场情绪。
中东供应担忧⽀撑油价，美元⾛软提振⻩⾦
受中东能源设施及油轮遇袭影响，市场对原油出⼝供应的担忧升温，油价有望迎来本⽉⾸次周
线上涨。布伦特原油涨1%⾄92.36美元每桶。

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking
the cash rate after the November increase. Since then, our
conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we
will publish the November Market Outlook with revised
forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing

market have all been revised down in 2027.
Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for
calendar 2027 more decisively inside the RBA’s 2–3% target
range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment
rate forecast has been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on
this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash rate from
here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.

A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The
housing market has undershot our forecasts over the past
couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been
coming in under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan
inquiries have taken another leg down in October after the
September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior
months, with the additional impact of the November rate hike
still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer.

Consumer income and spending have so far been quite
resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest
rates. But our Westpac Card Tracker is starting to show that
discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite
weak. We would not over-emphasise this point, but it does
imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB)

meeting, business survey data has shown weaker conditions
and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show
further evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-
labour cost shock that is not being fully passed through to
consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil
and fuel prices are already materially lower than the track
assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-
fourth lower than on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts

were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of
fuel costs to other prices, as well as easing consumer inflation
perceptions and expectations.
An RBA peak of greater conviction
Forecast changes
• A full update to our forecasts has been published in our
November Market Outlook.
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
In the media conference following the November meeting,
Governor Bullock flagged that it was a good time to “take

stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the
market read this as a signal that the MPB would pause at the
December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to
downside risks than the internal members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is
looking even less likely given signs in the data and surveys.
The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and

senior officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a
downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though,
a rate hike in 2027 would require either a material new
inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would
constitute a policy error on the part of the RBA (something
that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the talents
and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with

the dissenting voters in the MPB that the downside risks need
more attention.
Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to
February, we think there will be more evidence that the
domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation
outlook with it.
“A deeper housing downturn and early signs of
cracks in the consumer sector point to an even
softer 2027."

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
the labour market while showing significant concern over
the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the
passthrough of energy and technology input inflation. His
conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the monthly

rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward
supporting an increase in our policy rate of 25 basis points
at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI
inflation data out later today are similar to the past two
monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent
or even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also
kept the focus on inflation risks and stated or implied the FOMC

needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s
updates, pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January
meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by September. We believe
this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core
inflation ex shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-
month annualised basis and the labour market is balanced; but
it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate,

US households are under pressure from the cost of living, and
their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing
costs. Highlighting this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in
October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and
2010. Our November Market Outlook provide a full update on
our expectations for the US and the world.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index

fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest reading since
late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of
severe economic stress. Renewed cost-of-living and interest
rate pressures were front and centre following another lift
in inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the
RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage holders are feeling
the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group
falling to a record low. Consumers have become noticeably

more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the
relevant sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its
long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business
survey showed confidence slipped further to –15 in October as
conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic.
Of concern for the outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also
the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow
materially faster than selling prices, further compressing
already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter
financial conditions can jolt activity. The value of new dwelling
finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly
decline in the series’ history, taking approvals 25% lower year-
to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined

with the housing tax changes announced in May. Owner-
occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home
buyers proving particularly sensitive to the deterioration in
affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum
across the domestic economy, consistent with Westpac’s
expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside
activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our

expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the consensus
view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the
result, but core inflation was also marginally stronger than
forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the
past four months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look
to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost inflation through.
Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace,
gaining 0.3% in October.

The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated
by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor Waller prior to the
release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

### Page 4

4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Post election, where are we left?
This week markets and the economy needed to digest
the General Election results, as well as data on how the
economy is tracking through the final months of the year. A
centre-right coalition looks more likely to ultimately prevail,

although it may be a while before we know for sure and
longer again before a stable government emerges. Watch
this space as paths to victory for the centre-left remain.
Economic data are consistent with a still-strong external
sector but a weaker domestically-generated growth
impulse. Our upcoming Economic Overview next week will
examine these trends in more depth.
The real star of the show this week was the outcome of the
2026 General Election, which delivered an election night lead

to the centre-right coalition of the National, ACT and NZ First
parties. The preliminary result was a 67-57 seat margin in an
enlarged 131 seat parliament. Hence the centre-right won on
election night with a slender one-seat margin, given that 66
seats are required to form a majority. Financial markets and
the business sector generally cheered this outcome given their
preference for a centre-right government. However, the margins
are tight and there is the potential for the result to switch around

when the final vote count becomes available on November 27.
National leads Labour by narrow margins in two electorates
(Wairarapa and Hamilton East) and could lose those seats
when all is said and done. Also, special votes tend to swing
more to the left than the right historically and hence could
boost the centre-left bloc. The star performer of the election
was the Opportunity Party which debuted in parliament with
7 seats. The centre-left would require their support to get

over the line in all scenarios, whereas the centre-right might or
might not need them depending on how the special votes and
final electorate results play out. There seems a clearer path to
victory for the centre-right – especially if they can come to an
agreement with Opportunity – but clients should be wary of
complacency (which we note may be a risk).
A further complication is that the election left the incumbent
National Party in a much-weakened position as their coalition

partners picked up seats at their expense, and because their
much-reduced share of the party vote (29.2%) meant that
no National Party list MPs made it into parliament. Several
seasoned and senior government ministers needed a list seat
to make it into the new government including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. Even if the final results confirm a centre-right
coalition win, there will be fierce debate within the coalition
on which party staffs key ministerial roles. ACT party leader

David Seymour is making a strong play for Finance Minister,
which Prime Minister Christopher Luxon is fiercely resisting.
There is the potential for tough and protracted coalition
negotiations to play out well into the Christmas period.
Indeed, I spoke on a post-election panel with an ex-National
Party senior minister this week who expressed the view that
it might not be until Waitangi Day (February 6) before a new
government is formed. Should anything like this play out then

the implications for economic and market uncertainty could
be significant. The initial relief rally in the New Zealand dollar
might prove premature in that scenario.
For interest rate markets, the main conclusion drawn is that as
a centre-left government seems a lower probability prospect,
then so also is the likelihood of a return to a ‘dual mandate’
for the RBNZ. Chief Economist Paul Conway made remarks
this week suggesting ongoing concern with inflation risks,

suggesting he is still in the hike camp at this stage. This has
seen markets firm up short-term OCR expectations such that a
25bp OCR increase in December is now seen as high probability
(85-90% currently). Bond markets cheered the prospect of
potentially tighter fiscal policy that could come with a centre-
right government. The spread between the 10-year government
bond and swap spread narrowed 4 basis points over the week
and the 10-year government bond yield differential to the US

fell to -49 basis points – the most negative since 2019.
Economic data showed further evidence of the two-speed
economy that is showing above-trend growth yet at the same
time feels quite unsatisfying to most voters. The low exchange
rate, generally robust global demand and tight supply
conditions in soft commodity markets have been delivering
consistently strong export returns for a couple of years now.
This week we saw another very strong GDT Pulse auction that

propelled whole milk powder futures higher – up some 20-25%
on year ago levels in USD terms. Significant upside risk for our
2026/27 Farmgate Milk Price forecast of $9.75/kgMS now exists,
which we will be delving into more deeply in our Economic
Overview to be released on Wednesday. The manufacturing
sector appears to remain in a solid state, with the manufacturing
PMI slipping a touch in October to 54.9 from 56.3 in September.
Short-term visitor arrivals have resumed their prior strong

uptrend, growing almost 3% for a second consecutive month
in September and almost 10% higher than a year earlier.
While arrivals from key European markets remain well below
year-earlier levels – likely reflecting the disruption to travel
through key Middle Eastern hubs along with higher jet fuel
prices – this has been more than offset by a very sharp lift in
arrivals from China. Indeed, Chinese arrivals in September
were almost two-thirds higher than the same month last year.

Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

### Page 5

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
And in the September quarter, arrivals from China surpassed
their pre-pandemic level for the first time. The number of
Australian citizens visiting New Zealand grew just under 6%yr
in September, but growth momentum from New Zealand’s
number one source market (a 30% share) does appear to have

slowed somewhat in recent months.
Less positive trends were evident in the more domestically
orientated areas of the economy. The most concrete indicator
of the NZ economy – concrete poured – dropped back a
touch in the September quarter, while consumer spending
in fell 0.7% in October. We have concerns on the quality
of the consumer spending data given that bank-sourced
data indicates a rise in consumer spending in October. But
nonetheless it’s probably reasonable to infer that higher fuel

costs in October crimped household budgets.
Housing market data for October were also week. House sales
fell and are down almost 22% y/y, while house prices fell in
October for the fifth consecutive month to be down almost
2% y/y. The weak performance of the housing market is likely
another factor weighing on consumer spending and residential
investment. That’s another topic we will be taking a look at in
next week’s Economic Overview.
Net migration flows for September confirm still modest

population growth. Arrivals of non-New Zealand citizens
continues to drift higher while departures declined a touch.
The net outflow of New Zealanders remains relatively high but
is declining, as signs of firmer employment growth emerge in
New Zealand at the same time as the Australian labour market
shows signs of weakening.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Employment Change (000s)
Nov 19, Last: –39.3, Westpac f/c: 30
Market f/c: 27.5, Range: 9.9 to 42
Employment fell by 39.3k in September, well below expectations
for a 20k lift. Some of this weakness may reflect an unwind of
a possible residual seasonality stemming from recent changes

to the survey design, which saw August come in stronger.
Under the monthly noise, the labour market is loosening
gradually as jobs growth runs slower than population growth.
While some leading indicators point to near-term stability
– such as job ads – business surveys and detail within the
employment data point to some downside risk.
For October, we have pencilled in a lift in employment of +30k,
which would see employment growth hold steady on a three-

month average basis.
AUS: Nov Westpac Housing Sentiment (index)
Nov 17, Last: 85.3
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index that appears in our
regular Housing Pulse reports will now be published on a
monthly basis a week after Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Sentiment is released. The Index is a composite
based on housing-related sentiment measures from the survey
and has proven to be an extremely useful predictor of turning
points in housing markets, both nationally and across states.

At 85.3, the Oct monthly Index read was holding near historic
lows, well below the long run avg of 100. The Nov update will
capture reactions to the RBA’s latest interest rate rise and the
deepening and broadening housing downturn. The release
will also include results from two new questions added to
the regular survey on consumers’ property buying and selling
intentions over the next twelve months.
AUS: Q3 Wage Price Index (%qtr)
Nov 18, Last: 0.8, Westpac f/c: 1.0

Market f/c: 1.0, Range: 0.9 to 1.1
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 0.8% in Q2, leaving annual
growth at 3.2%. Private sector wages continued to decelerate,
in line with a softer labour market, while public sector wages
provided a bit of an offset.
The Fair Work Commission's (FWC) 2026 Annual Wage Review
handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award
wages. This will have a noticeable impact on the Q3 WPI, as the
wage increases stemming from the review are typically passed

on during the September quarter.
We have pencilled in a rise of 1.0% for Q3, lifting the annual
pace to 3.4%yr. Detail on wages growth by bargaining stream
will be closely watched for evidence of broader diffusion
beyond arrangements directly linked to the FWC's decision.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Unemployment Rate (%)
Nov 19, Last: 4.7, Westpac f/c: 4.7
Market f/c: 4.7, Range: 4.6 to 4.8
Once again, the surprise in employment coincided with a surprise
in labour force participation, which fell 0.2ppts to 66.8% from
its near cycle high of 67.0% in August. This translated to a 26.2k

decline in the size of the labour force, which was less than the
drop in employment. As a result, the unemployment rate ticked
slightly higher in September, from 4.65% to 4.72% (although it
was steady at 4.7% to one decimal place), a new cycle high.
For October, we have the participation rate steady at 66.8%,
which together with the forecast rise in employment, would
see the unemployment rate also hold steady at 4.7%. Early
signs of rising retrenchments are a key signal to watch over the

coming months, especially if new hiring across other areas of
the economy starts to dry up.
NZ: Oct Selected Consumer Prices
Nov 17
Stats NZ’s monthly Selected Prices provides an update on
around half of the full CPI, including many of the more volatile
items. In terms of the big groups, we’re expecting a 0.1%
rise in overall food prices, with fresh produce prices typically
soft at this time of year. We also expect continued softness
in housing rents. However, the key focus will be on transport

costs. Petrol prices are expected to be up 11%. And we’ll be
watching to see if high global fuel prices are contributing to
further increases in airfares after recent large increases. There
is related uncertainty around holiday accommodation costs
(which drive much of the month-to-month volatility in overall
prices).
NZ: Q4 RBNZ Survey of Expectations (%yr)
Nov 17, Inflation 2yrs Ahead, Last: 2.34
With an easing in headline inflation in the September

quarter, we’re likely to see some easing in near-term inflation
expectations (one year ahead). However, longer term inflation
expectations, particularly the closely-watched two years
ahead measure, are likely to remain broadly steady. While
interest rates are moving higher, businesses are continuing to
report pressures on operating costs. In addition, despite their
recent easing, oil prices remain elevated. The only time longer-
term inflation expectations have moved materially below 2.3%

was in the mid-2010s. That followed an extended period when
inflation was running below 2%.

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

Document 300: Cliff Notes_ in pursuit of future gain (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ in pursuit of future gain (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: in pursuit of future gain
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:02 November 27 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
The main event in Australia this week was the October CPI report which surprised modestly to the
upside, headline inflation rising 0.5% (4.9%yr). The main drivers were auto fuel (+6.2%), domestic
holiday travel (+4.5%) and new dwelling costs (+0.8%). Fuelled by some project home builders

reducing discounts, the increase in new dwelling costs was noteworthy; however, subdued demand
should see discounting return. More broadly, underlying inflation continued to accelerate. Trimmed

### Page 2

mean inflation gained 0.5% in the month and 3.8%yr, a cycle high that breaks the 3.5–3.6%yr range
held since May. The six-month annualised pulse was stronger still at 4.3%yr.
As noted by Westpac Chief Economist Luci Ellis following the release, October likely marks the peak
for inflation, but the upside surprise in dwellings and other early-quarter components skews the risks to
our Q4 trimmed mean estimate of 0.9%qtr up. The RBA is unlikely to relax its focus on price risks until

well into the new year.
This week, Australia also received two partial indicators for investment ahead of next Wednesday’s Q3
GDP report.
Construction activity was in line with Westpac’s expectation, up 0.9% in Q3 and 5.8% over the year.
The detail shows a clear rotation in activity: growth in infrastructure work moderating (1.3%yr) as major
public projects roll off; while momentum in residential (9.3%yr) and non-residential construction
(10.2%yr) pick up. Further, a strong pipeline of projects remains in place, ready to soak up any

additional capacity released by public infrastructure completions.
Private CAPEX subsequently surprised to the upside, up 12.2% in Q3, surpassing Westpac’s top-of-
the-market forecast of 10%. The headline result was driven by another very strong showing for data
centre-related investment, represented by a 184% surge in machinery and equipment spending in
information media and telecommunications. There are also signs of a nascent uptrend in investment

forming across other sectors, including utilities, financial and insurance services, and retail trade. At
16%yr, real (inflation-adjusted) capex plans for 2026-27 are the strongest since the mining investment
boom.
Ahead of next Wednesday’s release, our Q3 GDP preview will be published later today on Westpac IQ.
Offshore this week, the data released corroborated known themes. Ahead of Thanksgiving, US Q3
GDP growth was revised up from 3.4% annualised to 4.3% in the second estimate. Underlying the

result was stronger growth in personal consumption, revised up from 3.8% annualised to 4.2%, and a
broad-based upgrade for business investment. That said, monthly PCE data suggests consumer
momentum waned at quarter end, with real spending up just 0.1% in September and unchanged in
October despite an acceleration in nominal personal income growth (from 0.3% to 0.7%).
The Federal Reserve's Beige Book remains more downbeat than the hard data. "Eight of the twelve

Federal Reserve Districts reported growth in the slight to moderate range, while four Districts reported
no change. Consumer spending was little changed, as households remained price sensitive and were
increasingly selective in discretionary purchases." Given weak population growth and uncertainty over
momentum outside the tech sector, employers are " focused on utilizing existing staff more efficiently”,
“with many firms reporting stable head counts and hiring primarily to replace for attrition”. These

findings suggest that, should the pace of expansion in AI infrastructure plateau or turn, US GDP could
quickly return near trend.
The market’s mood continues to depend not only on the data to hand but also ongoing threats to
Middle East oil supply, Houthi rebels struck Saudi assets again this week with limited effect, and

### Page 3

Related articles
expectations for the AI buildout, which this week was dominated by news around NVIDIA. US trade
policy also remains a threat, the Trump administration this week announcing plans for a fresh round of
tariffs on China, the European Union and several other major trading partners to replace previous
levies struck down by the Supreme Court. China’s 20% tariff could double to 40% should trade
negotiations break down. India may also face a 25% tariff if it fails to reach a trade agreement with the

US. Uncertainty is likely to persist on each front well into 2027.

Document 334: GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Stocks Are Cheaper Everywhere

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Stocks Are Cheaper Everywhere.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-22
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Stocks Are Cheaper Everywhere
Date:    8 hours ago
GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Stocks Are Cheaper Everywhere

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKET CALL:
Stocks Are Cheaper Everywhere
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 22 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Gaël Gaborel - OrbisTerrae / Unsplash

### Page 2

The AI selloff went global last week. Investors fled to government bonds,
sending yields lower almost everywhere. Global profits are still booming,
yet valuations fell further. We see the selloff as a buying opportunity.
South Korean stocks are pricing in an earnings bust that analysts don't
see coming. In Japan, hotter inflation is raising the odds of a BOJ rate
hike. Here's more:
I. Stock Market Performance
Nearly every country ETF fell last week (chart). Taiwan was hit hardest,

down 23.2% in dollars, followed by South Korea down 13.7%. India was
the only gainer, up 2.8%. The US fell 8.6%, slightly less than the All
Country World ex-US ETF's 9.0% decline.
Stay Home and Go Global are now running neck and neck this year. The
ratios of the US MSCI to the All Country World ex-US MSCI, in dollars and
local currency, have been basically flat since the start of this year (chart).
On October 18, we decided to lower Go Global and raise Stay Home to

market weights.

### Page 3

The US MSCI's share of the All Country World MSCI's market cap edged
down to 65.1% from 65.3% last week (chart). Europe's share edged up to
13.7%, and the EM MSCI edged down to 11.1%. Japan's is just 4.9%.
II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
US and overseas forward earnings both remain at record highs (chart).
The global stock market selloff hasn't dented analysts' optimistic earnings
estimates so far.

### Page 4

Global profits are still booming. Forward profit margins are at or near
record highs almost everywhere (chart). Canada's is 17.5%, and the US's
is 16.9%. Japan's has climbed to 10.5%, rapidly approaching the EMU's
10.6%. Might profit margins be reflecting greater AI-driven productivity?
Maybe.
Valuations fell sharply again last week. The US MSCI's forward P/E is
down to 17.7 (chart). The EM MSCI trades at just 7.9, its lowest since the
Great Financial Crisis. We said valuations were at or near a bottom. They

went lower. We still think the panic selling will abate soon.

### Page 5

The US valuation premium is narrowing. The All Country World ex-US
MSCI's forward P/E is 11.2 (chart). The gap with the US has narrowed to
6.5 points from a peak of 9.5 in December 2024.
III. Bonds
Bonds rallied worldwide as investors fled stocks (chart). The US 10-year
Treasury yield fell 24bps to 4.92% from 5.16% a week earlier. The UK's
5.25% is still the highest in the group, followed by Australia's 4.96%.
Japan's yield eased to 2.78%.

### Page 6

Eurozone government bond yield spreads over German bunds are
widening again. The spread between French and German 10-year yields
rose to 157.4bps from 154.2bps (chart). Italy's spread is 110.3bps, and
Greece's is 95.9bps. France remains the weak link.
IV. Dollar
The dollar has been a safe haven during the AI selloff. The DXY rose to
103.8, up from an intraday low of 101.1 on November 4 (chart). We've
been expecting the dollar to remain above the lower end of its upward-

sloping channel. So far, so good.

### Page 7

V. Gold & Commodities
Gold's selling panic continues. The price fell to $3,555 per ounce on
Friday, its lowest level since September 2025 (chart). We still expect
support around $3,400. We pushed our $5,000 year-end 2026 target to
the end of 2027. This is not one of our high-conviction forecasts right now.
Commodity prices fell broadly last week (chart). Copper dropped 9.9% to
$5.48 a pound, as it continues to track the AI trade. Brent crude oil eased

to $92.57 a barrel.

### Page 8

VI. Focus On South Korea & Japan
South Korea's stock market has crashed even as its earnings continue to
soar. The South Korea MSCI's forward earnings is up 325.1% ytd, while
its stock price is up just 26.8% (chart). The price was up about 150% in
June. That has cut the forward P/E by 70.2% this year to a mere 3.2, the
lowest on record. Investors are betting analysts' estimates are too high
based on the reasonable concern that a slowdown in the AI buildout will

depress the memory chip prices of Samsung and SK Hynix.
South Korean exports tell a different story. They soared 93.9% y/y in
October to a record, led by shipments to China and the US (chart). We
see the US backlash against data centers as a speed bump, not a
roadblock, for Asia's chipmakers too.

### Page 9

Japan's inflation is running hot again. Headline CPI rose to 2.4% y/y in
October, above the Bank of Japan's 2.0% target (chart). The jump came
largely from the removal of gasoline subsidies.
The BOJ's policy rate is just 1.25% (chart). The odds of a December hike
are around 65%.

### Page 10

Japan's stock market has moved the same way as Korea's, just less
dramatically. Japan's MSCI forward earnings is up 31.3% ytd, but its stock
price is up just 6.6% (chart).

《AI發債引發債務可持續性擔憂》
近期公債殖利率攀高引發市場對AI融資需求以及
企業償債能力的擔憂，AI產業鏈信用利差與CDS
擴大，不過多數大型科技企業多為投資級，美國
投資級AI產業鏈信用利差水準仍未突破今年高點；
此外統計今年以來大量發債的美國投資級債發行
人(淨發行量100億美元以上)共有17家，其中17%
的發行人信評獲調升，33%被下調，50%不變，
反映債務增加尚未造成普遍的債信壓力。

差擴大主要反映市場對AI經濟效益的重新評估，以
及對評級較低、資本密集度較高的發行人要求更高
的風險溢酬，並非潛在的信用風險事件或整體信用
基本面惡化，不過仍須留意目前總體經濟環境對於
信用評級較低的發行人而言更容易受到長期高利率
和成本壓力的影響，高低評等發行人的市場表現分
歧或將加大。
另一方面，回顧2026年第三季美投資級債評等變動
不多，2026年迄今的評級上調/下調比率為2.3倍；
雖然這一比率低於2022-2024年期間強勁的升評趨

勢，但高於2025年，且已是第六年大於一倍，反映
投資級債整體信用環境維持良好。

狀況良好、資產品質未顯著
惡化
《歐洲信用債市受法國財政疑慮波及》
歐洲信用市場雖受AI相關債券波動影響較小，但因
能源價格高企，加上近期法國主權和財政風險加劇，
歐洲信用債市十月份表現也有不小波動，尤其是非
投資級債信用利差一度擴大逾60個基點。歐洲銀行
債是各產業信用債主權風險曝險較高的類別，因歐
洲銀行多數不及美國銀行營運遍及全球且業務多元，
此外也持有不少主權債資產，十月份市場表現也反
映此一狀況，主權債的波動更加重了歐洲銀行債的
跌幅，法國銀行債利差擴大幅度最為明顯，所幸法
國主要銀行包括法國巴黎銀行以及興業銀行3Q26獲
利狀況良好，即使經營環境惡化，從不良貸款率與

信用風險成本等資產品質指標來看法國巴黎銀行持
平前季、法國興業銀行僅微幅上升。
目前市場關注的焦點仍是法國財政整頓的路徑，法
國目前的債務負擔約佔GDP的118%至119%，高盛
預計，如果沒有更有效的財政調整，到2030年初負
債比率將進一步上升至接近125%，如要穩定債務
佔GDP的比重，法國需要實現約佔GDP 1%的財政
盈餘，但目前的財政赤字約佔2.5% GDP。法國政
府當局可能在年底前通過一項撙節的預算案，若沒
通過法國主權債與信用債可能進一步惡化，但即使
通過法國主權債利率要大幅回落的機會仍不大，可
能要到2027年法國大選後主權債才有機會趨於穩定，
屆時法國銀行債或有布局機會。
歐洲可供吸收損失銀行債信用利差(基點)
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10
法國銀行債受主權債重新定價帶來的衝擊影響

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
• 高利率持續下信用基本面都
已 出 現分 化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱
• 衡量整體債信風險仍是歐洲
較美國為高
《非投資級債美國仍優於歐洲》
儘管目前不論是美國還是歐洲的非投資級債在高利
率持續下信用基本面都已出現分化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱，但衡量整體債信風險仍是
歐洲較美國為高；原因包括歐洲的債務到期牆較美
國明顯，且美國非投資級債2028年前到期的債券分
佈在相對廣泛的行業，而當前的賤售債券主要集中
在媒體、原物料與電信、非必需消費等特定領域，
上述較少重疊降低落入賤售債券利差區間的發行人
同時面臨債務到期後償債或再融資令違約風險增加
的可能。另一方面，歐洲非投資級債的到期牆更為
陡峭，且市場利率與2028年前到期債券的票面利率

差距大於美非投資級債，反映再融資壓力更鉅，尤
其是歐洲CCC等級。
分析歐洲非投資級債各產業債務到期狀況可發現
2028年前到期債務產業相對集中，且易受景氣影響
的非必需消費在到期債務與賤售債券都佔有一定比
例，另外槓桿率本就較高的電信業佔2028年前到期
債務9%，融資成本大幅上漲恐令這些發行人債信體
質進一步惡化。
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

經回到輝達上班。

營收年增17%、Mac營收年增24%，因公司分別在

至5,730萬支，顯示即使消費者面臨通膨及利率波
動，富裕階層仍較不受影響、仍能支持高階手機市
場。

2027財年iPhone銷量較2026財年下滑，但平均售

價提升及折疊手機iPhone Duo的早期需求強勁將

美元影響。儘管3Q26三星手機銷售仍有支撐，但
小米、OPPO 、vivo 等 中國品 牌手 機出 貨量 年減
29.2%、34.5%、31.5%，銷售量大幅下滑，顯示
在記憶體價格持續攀升的情況下，消費者對中低階
手機的價格敏感度較高。展望後市，在通膨與利率
仍高的情況下，預估1H27中低階手機銷售仍相對
不樂觀。
